# Shared state policy. GUI dispatch resolves one state; no action searches random enemy states.
SRC_local_resolve_state = {
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_328 } 328 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_408 } 408 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_409 } 409 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_560 } 560 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_561 } 561 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_610 } 610 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_657 } 657 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_714 } 714 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_715 } 715 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_716 } 716 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_717 } 717 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_745 } 745 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_761 } 761 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_845 } 845 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_846 } 846 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_847 } 847 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_848 } 848 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_849 } 849 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_850 } 850 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_1112 } 1112 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_1123 } 1123 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_1126 } 1126 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
    else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_dongbei_front_selected_1218 } 1218 = { save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state } }
}

SRC_la_compute_state = {
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = SRC_la_network_level } } set_variable = { SRC_la_network_level = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = SRC_la_administration_level } } set_variable = { SRC_la_administration_level = 0 } }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_status = 0 }
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_stable_src = yes } set_variable = { SRC_la_status = 1 } }
    else_if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_stable_gps = yes } set_variable = { SRC_la_status = 3 } }
    else_if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_contested = yes } set_variable = { SRC_la_status = 2 } }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_type_industrial = 0 }
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_industrial = yes } set_variable = { SRC_la_type_industrial = 1 } }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_type_city = 0 }
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_city = yes } set_variable = { SRC_la_type_city = 1 } }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_type_border = 0 }
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_border = yes } set_variable = { SRC_la_type_border = 1 } }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_type_transport = 0 }
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_transport = yes } set_variable = { SRC_la_type_transport = 1 } }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_type_rural = 0 }
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_rural = yes } set_variable = { SRC_la_type_rural = 1 } }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_worker_scale = 1 }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_worker_cost = 50 }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_underground_scale = 1 }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_underground_cost = 40 }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_revgov_scale = 1 }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_revgov_cost = 60 }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_peasant_scale = 1 }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_peasant_cost = 45 }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_student_scale = 1 }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_student_cost = 45 }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_business_scale = 1 }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_business_cost = 50 }
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_industrial = yes }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_worker_scale = 1.5 }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_business_scale = 1.25 }
    }
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_city = yes }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_student_scale = 1.5 }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_underground_scale = 1.25 }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_business_scale = 1.25 }
    }
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_border = yes }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_underground_scale = 1.25 }
    }
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_transport = yes }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_worker_scale = 1.25 }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_underground_scale = 1.25 }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_business_scale = 1.25 }
    }
    if = { limit = { OR = { SRC_la_state_border = yes SRC_la_state_rural = yes } } multiply_variable = { SRC_la_peasant_scale = 1.5 } }
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_la_worker_scale min = 1 max = 1.75 }
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_la_underground_scale min = 1 max = 1.75 }
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_la_revgov_scale min = 1 max = 1.75 }
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_la_peasant_scale min = 1 max = 1.75 }
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_la_student_scale min = 1 max = 1.75 }
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_la_business_scale min = 1 max = 1.75 }
    # Existing organisation amplifies a later action; it never changes its role.
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_stable_gps = yes check_variable = { SRC_la_network_level > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_la_worker_cost = 10 }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_la_student_cost = 10 }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_worker_scale = 1.2 }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_revgov_scale = 1.2 }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_peasant_scale = 1.2 }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_student_scale = 1.2 }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_business_scale = 1.25 }
    }
    if = { limit = { has_state_flag = SRC_la_student_publicity }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_la_underground_cost = 10 }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_la_revgov_cost = 10 }
    }
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_stable_src = yes check_variable = { SRC_la_administration_level > 0 } }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_la_peasant_cost = 10 }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_peasant_scale = 1.2 }
    }
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_stable_src = yes has_state_flag = SRC_la_secret_committee NOT = { has_state_flag = SRC_la_committee_discount_used } }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_la_revgov_cost = 30 }
    }
    if = { limit = { has_state_flag = SRC_la_secret_committee } multiply_variable = { SRC_la_underground_scale = 1.2 } }
    if = { limit = { has_state_flag = SRC_la_rural_contacts } multiply_variable = { SRC_la_underground_scale = 1.1 } }
    if = { limit = { has_state_flag = SRC_la_student_contacts } multiply_variable = { SRC_la_underground_scale = 1.25 } }
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_contested = yes has_state_flag = SRC_la_front_publicity }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_worker_scale = 1.1 }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_underground_scale = 1.1 }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_revgov_scale = 1.1 }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_peasant_scale = 1.1 }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_business_scale = 1.1 }
    }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_underground_cooldown = 45 }
    if = { limit = { has_state_flag = SRC_la_student_contacts } set_variable = { SRC_la_underground_cooldown = 30 } }
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_la_worker_cost min = 10 max = 100 }
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_la_underground_cost min = 10 max = 100 }
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_la_revgov_cost min = 10 max = 100 }
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_la_peasant_cost min = 10 max = 100 }
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_la_student_cost min = 10 max = 100 }
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_la_business_cost min = 10 max = 100 }
    # Preview uses its own variables; active modifiers keep their click-time snapshots.
    SRC_la_preview_worker = yes
    SRC_la_preview_underground = yes
    SRC_la_preview_revgov = yes
    SRC_la_preview_peasant = yes
    SRC_la_preview_student = yes
    SRC_la_preview_business = yes
    SRC = {
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_rifles = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_rifles }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_support = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_support }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_trucks = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_trucks }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_trains = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_trains }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_manpower = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_manpower }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_xp = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_xp }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_political = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_political }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_src_state_repair_speed_arms_factory_factor = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_src_state_repair_speed_arms_factory_factor }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_src_state_repair_speed_industrial_complex_factor = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_src_state_repair_speed_industrial_complex_factor }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_src_state_repair_speed_rail_way_factor = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_src_state_repair_speed_rail_way_factor }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_src_state_production_speed_arms_factory_factor = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_src_state_production_speed_arms_factory_factor }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_src_army_attack_factor = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_src_army_attack_factor }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_src_army_defence_factor = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_src_army_defence_factor }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_src_supply_consumption_factor = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_src_supply_consumption_factor }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_gps_supply_factor = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_gps_supply_factor }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_gps_state_repair_speed_rail_way_factor = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_gps_state_repair_speed_rail_way_factor }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_gps_local_factory_sabotage = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_gps_local_factory_sabotage }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_worker_days = PREV.SRC_la_preview_worker_days }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_underground_rifles = PREV.SRC_la_preview_underground_rifles }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_underground_support = PREV.SRC_la_preview_underground_support }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_underground_trucks = PREV.SRC_la_preview_underground_trucks }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_underground_trains = PREV.SRC_la_preview_underground_trains }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_underground_manpower = PREV.SRC_la_preview_underground_manpower }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_underground_xp = PREV.SRC_la_preview_underground_xp }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_underground_political = PREV.SRC_la_preview_underground_political }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_underground_src_local_intel_to_enemies = PREV.SRC_la_preview_underground_src_local_intel_to_enemies }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_underground_src_enemy_spy_negative_status_factor = PREV.SRC_la_preview_underground_src_enemy_spy_negative_status_factor }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_underground_src_recon_factor = PREV.SRC_la_preview_underground_src_recon_factor }
        set_variable = { SRC_local_underground_gps_army_defence_factor = PREV.SRC_la_preview_underground_gps_army_defence_factor }

SRC_local_refresh_selected = {
    SRC_dongbei_front_select_default = yes
    SRC_local_resolve_state = yes
    set_variable = { SRC_local_status = 0 }
    event_target:SRC_local_action_state = { SRC_la_compute_state = yes }
    add_to_variable = { SRC_unified_gui_dirty = 0.01 }
}

# All immediate resource gains are computed in state scope, rounded once, then paid to SRC.
SRC_la_grant_resources = {
    SRC = {
        if = { limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { SRC_la_reward_rifles > 0 } } }
            add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = PREV.SRC_la_reward_rifles }
        }
        if = { limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { SRC_la_reward_support > 0 } } }
            add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = PREV.SRC_la_reward_support }
        }
        if = { limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { SRC_la_reward_trucks > 0 } } }
            add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment amount = PREV.SRC_la_reward_trucks }
        }
        if = { limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { SRC_la_reward_trains > 0 } } }
            add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment amount = PREV.SRC_la_reward_trains }
        }
        if = { limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { SRC_la_reward_manpower > 0 } } }
            add_manpower = PREV.SRC_la_reward_manpower
        }
        if = { limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { SRC_la_reward_xp > 0 } } }
            army_experience = PREV.SRC_la_reward_xp
        }
    }
}
SRC_la_clear_rewards = {
    set_variable = { SRC_la_reward_rifles = 0 }
    set_variable = { SRC_la_reward_support = 0 }

        set_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_src_land_reinforce_rate = 0.01 }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { SRC_la_status = 3 } }
        set_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_days = 45 }
        set_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_gps_supply_factor = -0.075 }
        set_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_gps_truck_attrition_factor = 0.1 }
        set_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_gps_state_repair_speed_rail_way_factor = -0.15 }
    }
    multiply_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_rifles = SRC_la_business_scale }
    round_variable = SRC_la_preview_business_rifles
    multiply_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_support = SRC_la_business_scale }
    round_variable = SRC_la_preview_business_support
    multiply_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_trucks = SRC_la_business_scale }
    round_variable = SRC_la_preview_business_trucks
    multiply_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_trains = SRC_la_business_scale }
    round_variable = SRC_la_preview_business_trains
    multiply_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_manpower = SRC_la_business_scale }
    round_variable = SRC_la_preview_business_manpower
    multiply_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_xp = SRC_la_business_scale }
    round_variable = SRC_la_preview_business_xp
    multiply_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_political = SRC_la_business_scale }
    round_variable = SRC_la_preview_business_political
    multiply_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_src_supply_factor = SRC_la_business_scale }
    multiply_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_src_supply_consumption_factor = SRC_la_business_scale }
    multiply_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_src_land_reinforce_rate = SRC_la_business_scale }
    multiply_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_gps_supply_factor = SRC_la_business_scale }
    multiply_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_gps_truck_attrition_factor = SRC_la_business_scale }
    multiply_variable = { SRC_la_preview_business_gps_state_repair_speed_rail_way_factor = SRC_la_business_scale }
}

SRC_la_worker_src_aftermath = {
    # Resources and timed local modifiers are the complete reward.
}

SRC_la_worker_contested_aftermath = {
    # Resources and timed local modifiers are the complete reward.
}

SRC_la_worker_gps_aftermath = {
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_industrial = yes } random = { chance = 15 SRC_la_minor_sabotage = yes } }
    else = { random = { chance = 10 SRC_la_minor_sabotage = yes } }
}

SRC_la_underground_src_aftermath = {
    set_state_flag = SRC_la_local_contacts
    set_state_flag = { flag = SRC_la_counterintelligence days = 45 value = 1 }
    SRC = { if = { limit = { country_exists = GPS } add_intel = { target = GPS army_intel = 1 } } }
}

SRC_la_underground_contested_aftermath = {
    set_state_flag = SRC_la_local_contacts
    SRC = { if = { limit = { country_exists = GPS } add_intel = { target = GPS army_intel = 2 } } }
}

SRC_la_underground_gps_aftermath = {
    add_to_variable = { SRC_la_network_level = 1 }
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_la_network_level min = 0 max = 2 }
    set_state_flag = SRC_la_local_contacts
    SRC = { if = { limit = { country_exists = GPS } add_intel = { target = GPS army_intel = 2 } } }
}

SRC_la_revgov_src_aftermath = {
    set_variable = { SRC_la_administration_level = 2 }
    set_state_flag = SRC_la_revolutionary_administration
    if = { limit = { has_state_flag = SRC_la_secret_committee NOT = { has_state_flag = SRC_la_committee_discount_used } } set_state_flag = SRC_la_committee_discount_used }
    SRC = {
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = SRC_la_stability_grants } } set_variable = { SRC_la_stability_grants = 0 } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { SRC_la_stability_grants < 4 } } add_stability = 0.005 add_to_variable = { SRC_la_stability_grants = 1 } }
    }
}

SRC_la_revgov_contested_aftermath = {
    set_state_flag = SRC_la_field_administration
    set_state_flag = { flag = SRC_la_field_administration_prepared days = 90 value = 1 }
}

SRC_la_revgov_gps_aftermath = {
    set_state_flag = SRC_la_secret_committee
}

SRC_la_peasant_src_aftermath = {
    # Resources and timed local modifiers are the complete reward.
}

SRC_la_peasant_contested_aftermath = {

SRC_la_student_src_aftermath = {
    set_state_flag = { flag = SRC_la_student_publicity days = 60 value = 1 }
    SRC = {
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = SRC_la_student_warsupport_grants } } set_variable = { SRC_la_student_warsupport_grants = 0 } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { SRC_la_student_warsupport_grants < 4 } } add_war_support = 0.005 add_to_variable = { SRC_la_student_warsupport_grants = 1 } }
        if = { limit = { has_war_with = GPS NOT = { SRC_wartime_army_has_final_spirit = yes } PREV = { NOT = { has_state_flag = SRC_la_student_training_granted } } }
            SRC_wartime_army_ensure_v2 = yes
            add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_political_stage = PREV.SRC_la_preview_student_political }
            SRC_wartime_army_refresh_spirit = yes
            SRC_check_wartime_army_stage_progress = yes
        }
    }
    set_state_flag = SRC_la_student_training_granted
}

SRC_la_student_contested_aftermath = {
    set_state_flag = { flag = SRC_la_front_publicity days = 30 value = 1 }
}

SRC_la_student_gps_aftermath = {
    set_state_flag = { flag = SRC_la_student_contacts days = 60 value = 1 }
    SRC = {
        if = { limit = { country_exists = GPS } add_intel = { target = GPS army_intel = 2 } }
        if = { limit = { has_country_flag = { flag = SRC_dongbei_activity_underground_cd days < 15 } } set_country_flag = { flag = SRC_dongbei_activity_underground_cd days = 15 value = 1 } }
    }
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_city = yes } random = { chance = 20 SRC_la_student_protest = yes } }
    else = { random = { chance = 10 SRC_la_student_protest = yes } }
}

    # Resources and timed local modifiers are the complete reward.

    # Resources and timed local modifiers are the complete reward.

SRC_la_minor_sabotage = {
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_stable_gps = yes }
        if = { limit = { infrastructure > 0 } damage_building = { type = infrastructure damage = 0.25 } }
        if = { limit = { arms_factory > 0 } damage_building = { type = arms_factory damage = 0.20 } }
        save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state
        SRC = { country_event = { id = SRC_local_actions.1 } }
    }
}
SRC_la_student_protest = {
    add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_la_student_protest_modifier scope = GPS days = 10 }
    save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state
    SRC = { country_event = { id = SRC_local_actions.2 } }
}

# A prepared administration survives a control change; it never awards manpower on a daily tick.
SRC_la_update_state_organisation = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = SRC_la_network_level } } set_variable = { SRC_la_network_level = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = SRC_la_administration_level } } set_variable = { SRC_la_administration_level = 0 } }
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_stable_src = yes }
        if = { limit = { OR = { has_state_flag = SRC_la_field_administration has_state_flag = SRC_la_secret_committee } NOT = { check_variable = { SRC_la_administration_level > 0 } } }
            set_variable = { SRC_la_administration_level = 1 }
            set_state_flag = SRC_la_revolutionary_administration
        }
        if = {
            limit = { has_state_flag = SRC_la_secret_committee NOT = { has_state_flag = SRC_la_liberation_received } }
            set_state_flag = SRC_la_liberation_received
            SRC = { add_timed_idea = { idea = SRC_la_liberation_order days = 30 } }
        }
        clr_state_flag = SRC_la_admin_contested_recovery
    }
    else_if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_contested = yes check_variable = { SRC_la_administration_level > 0 } NOT = { has_state_flag = SRC_la_admin_contested_recovery } }
        set_state_flag = SRC_la_admin_contested_recovery
        add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_la_admin_recovery_modifier scope = SRC days = 30 }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { SRC_la_state_stable_gps = yes } clr_state_flag = SRC_la_admin_contested_recovery }
    SRC_la_dispatch_cleanup = yes
}

SRC_local_try_worker = {
    SRC_local_refresh_selected = yes
    if = { limit = { SRC_la_can_worker = yes }
        set_variable = { SRC_la_pp_payment = SRC_local_worker_cost }
        multiply_variable = { SRC_la_pp_payment = -1 }
        add_political_power = SRC_la_pp_payment
        SRC_set_selected_bureau_worker_cd = yes
        event_target:SRC_local_action_state = { SRC_la_apply_worker_state = yes }
        SRC_local_refresh_selected = yes
    }
}

# One enemy sabotage attempt per month, paid by GPS. Counterintelligence has an actual consumer.
SRC_la_gps_counteraction = {
    if = { limit = { has_state_flag = SRC_la_counterintelligence GPS = { has_political_power > 24 } }
        GPS = { add_political_power = -25 }
        random = { chance = 5 SRC_la_gps_minor_sabotage = yes }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = SRC_la_counterintelligence } GPS = { has_political_power > 14 } }
        GPS = { add_political_power = -15 }
        random = { chance = 10 SRC_la_gps_minor_sabotage = yes }
    }
}
SRC_la_gps_minor_sabotage = {
    if = { limit = { infrastructure > 0 } damage_building = { type = infrastructure damage = 0.20 } }
    save_event_target_as = SRC_local_action_state
    SRC = { country_event = { id = SRC_local_actions.3 } }
}

    if = { limit = { NOT = { SRC_la_state_stable_src = yes } has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_la_underground_src_src_modifier_1218 scope = SRC } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_la_underground_src_src_modifier_1218 scope = SRC } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { SRC_la_state_stable_gps = yes } has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_la_underground_gps_gps_modifier_1218 scope = GPS } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_la_underground_gps_gps_modifier_1218 scope = GPS } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { SRC_la_state_stable_src = yes } has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_la_revgov_src_src_modifier_1218 scope = SRC } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_la_revgov_src_src_modifier_1218 scope = SRC } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { SRC_la_state_stable_gps = yes } has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_la_revgov_gps_gps_modifier_1218 scope = GPS } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_la_revgov_gps_gps_modifier_1218 scope = GPS } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { SRC_la_state_stable_src = yes } has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_la_peasant_src_src_modifier_1218 scope = SRC } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_la_peasant_src_src_modifier_1218 scope = SRC } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { SRC_la_state_stable_gps = yes } has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_la_peasant_gps_gps_modifier_1218 scope = GPS } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_la_peasant_gps_gps_modifier_1218 scope = GPS } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { SRC_la_state_stable_src = yes } has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_la_student_src_src_modifier_1218 scope = SRC } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_la_student_src_src_modifier_1218 scope = SRC } }

#============================================================
### merged from SRC_power_struggle_effects.txt

SRC_power_struggle_init_balance = {
    set_variable = { SRC_xianfeng = 1 }
    set_variable = { SRC_weiyuan = 1 }
    set_variable = { SRC_wenhe = 1 }
    SRC_power_struggle_init_central_satisfaction = yes
    SRC_power_struggle_normalize_balance = yes
}

SRC_power_struggle_init_central_satisfaction = {
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { SRC_central_support_ren > 0 } } }
        set_variable = { SRC_central_support_ren = 50 }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { SRC_central_support_li > 0 } } }
        set_variable = { SRC_central_support_li = 50 }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { SRC_central_support_chen > 0 } } }
        set_variable = { SRC_central_support_chen = 50 }
    }
    SRC_power_struggle_normalize_central_satisfaction = yes
}

SRC_power_struggle_normalize_central_satisfaction = {
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_central_support_ren min = 0 max = 100 }
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_central_support_li min = 0 max = 100 }
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_central_support_chen min = 0 max = 100 }
    add_to_variable = { SRC_power_struggle_gui_dirty = 0.01 }
}

SRC_power_struggle_normalize_balance = {
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_xianfeng min = 0 max = 100 }
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_weiyuan min = 0 max = 100 }
    clamp_variable = { var = SRC_wenhe min = 0 max = 100 }

    set_temp_variable = { SRC_power_total = SRC_xianfeng }
    add_to_temp_variable = { SRC_power_total = SRC_weiyuan }
    add_to_temp_variable = { SRC_power_total = SRC_wenhe }

    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { SRC_power_total < 0.01 } }
        set_variable = { SRC_xianfeng = 1 }
        set_variable = { SRC_weiyuan = 1 }
        set_variable = { SRC_wenhe = 1 }
        set_temp_variable = { SRC_power_total = 3 }
    }

    divide_variable = { SRC_xianfeng = SRC_power_total }
    multiply_variable = { SRC_xianfeng = 100 }
    divide_variable = { SRC_weiyuan = SRC_power_total }
    multiply_variable = { SRC_weiyuan = 100 }
    divide_variable = { SRC_wenhe = SRC_power_total }
    multiply_variable = { SRC_wenhe = 100 }

    SRC_power_struggle_calc_tangle_xy = yes
    if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_li_bureaucratic_reform_enacted }
        SRC_party_cooperation_recalculate = yes
    }
}

SRC_power_struggle_calc_tangle_xy = {
    set_temp_variable = { SRC_power_total = SRC_xianfeng }
    add_to_temp_variable = { SRC_power_total = SRC_weiyuan }
    add_to_temp_variable = { SRC_power_total = SRC_wenhe }

    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { SRC_power_total < 0.01 } }
        set_temp_variable = { SRC_power_total = 100 }
        set_variable = { SRC_xianfeng = 33.333 }
        set_variable = { SRC_weiyuan = 33.333 }
        set_variable = { SRC_wenhe = 33.334 }
    }

    # Triangle marker top-left inside src_power_detail_panel.
    # The triangle art is placed at 20,42; coordinates below offset the
    # 24x24 marker so the pin center sits on each triangle vertex.
    # Ren:   63,418
    # Li:   258,54
    # Chen: 453,418
    # Values are barycentric weights and are normalized by SRC_power_total.
    set_variable = { SRC_power_tangle_x = SRC_xianfeng }
    multiply_variable = { SRC_power_tangle_x = 63 }
    set_temp_variable = { SRC_power_li_x = SRC_weiyuan }
    multiply_temp_variable = { SRC_power_li_x = 258 }
    add_to_variable = { SRC_power_tangle_x = SRC_power_li_x }
    set_temp_variable = { SRC_power_chen_x = SRC_wenhe }
    multiply_temp_variable = { SRC_power_chen_x = 453 }
    add_to_variable = { SRC_power_tangle_x = SRC_power_chen_x }
    divide_variable = { SRC_power_tangle_x = SRC_power_total }

    set_variable = { SRC_power_tangle_y = SRC_xianfeng }
    multiply_variable = { SRC_power_tangle_y = 418 }
    set_temp_variable = { SRC_power_li_y = SRC_weiyuan }
    multiply_temp_variable = { SRC_power_li_y = 54 }
    add_to_variable = { SRC_power_tangle_y = SRC_power_li_y }
    set_temp_variable = { SRC_power_chen_y = SRC_wenhe }
    multiply_temp_variable = { SRC_power_chen_y = 418 }
    add_to_variable = { SRC_power_tangle_y = SRC_power_chen_y }
    divide_variable = { SRC_power_tangle_y = SRC_power_total }

    add_to_variable = { SRC_power_struggle_gui_dirty = 0.01 }
}

SRC_power_struggle_refresh_psc_gui = {
    add_to_variable = { SRC_power_struggle_gui_dirty = 0.01 }
}

SRC_power_struggle_select_ren = {
    set_country_flag = SRC_power_struggle_selected_ren
    clr_country_flag = SRC_power_struggle_selected_li
    clr_country_flag = SRC_power_struggle_selected_chen
    SRC_power_struggle_refresh_psc_gui = yes
}

SRC_power_struggle_add_selected_support = {

        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_weiyuan = 2 }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_wenhe = 2 }
        add_to_variable = { SRC_central_support_ren = 6 }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_central_support_li = 3 }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_central_support_chen = 3 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_power_struggle_selected_li }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_xianfeng = 2 }
        add_to_variable = { SRC_weiyuan = 4 }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_wenhe = 2 }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_central_support_ren = 3 }
        add_to_variable = { SRC_central_support_li = 6 }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_central_support_chen = 3 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_power_struggle_selected_chen }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_xianfeng = 2 }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_weiyuan = 2 }
        add_to_variable = { SRC_wenhe = 4 }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_central_support_ren = 3 }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_central_support_li = 3 }
        add_to_variable = { SRC_central_support_chen = 6 }

    SRC_power_struggle_normalize_balance = yes
}

#============================================================
### merged from SRC_wartime_army_stages.txt
#============================================================
# Chapter progression is independent of the four construction values.
# Recount completed work from durable flags, including pre-chapter saves.
SRC_wartime_army_recount_stage_work = {
	set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_one_completed = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_retain_officers_done } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_one_completed = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_party_cells_done } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_one_completed = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_soldier_committee_done } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_one_completed = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_supply_network_done } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_one_completed = 1 } }
	set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_two_completed = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_unified_revolutionary_designations } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_two_completed = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_crash_military_training_courses } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_two_completed = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_temporary_red_army_discipline } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_two_completed = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_armament_registry } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_two_completed = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_grassroots_cadres_from_pickets } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_two_completed = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_expanded_field_command } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_two_completed = 1 } }
	set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_three_completed = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_field_staff_office } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_three_completed = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_veteran_training_units } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_three_completed = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_field_political_work_system } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_three_completed = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_grassroots_battlefield_initiative } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_three_completed = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_field_repair_workshops } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_three_completed = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_railway_priority_for_military_transport } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_three_completed = 1 } }
	set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_five_completed = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_northeast_red_army_general_headquarters } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_five_completed = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_permanent_training_system } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_five_completed = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_completed_political_reorganisation } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_five_completed = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_unified_supply_department } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage_five_completed = 1 } }
	set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_values_at_30 = 0 }
	if = { limit = { OR = { check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_command_stage > 30 } check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_command_stage = 30 } } } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_values_at_30 = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { OR = { check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_training_stage > 30 } check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_training_stage = 30 } } } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_values_at_30 = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { OR = { check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_political_stage > 30 } check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_political_stage = 30 } } } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_values_at_30 = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { OR = { check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_supply_stage > 30 } check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_supply_stage = 30 } } } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_values_at_30 = 1 } }
}

SRC_wartime_army_complete_chapters = {
	set_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_summary_complete
	set_country_flag = SRC_army_reorganization_completed
	clr_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_stage_event_pending
	add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_gui_dirty = 0.01 }
}

# Old saves replay missing chapters in order without resetting metrics or choices.
SRC_wartime_army_ensure_chapters = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = SRC_wartime_army_stage } }
		set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage = 1 }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_summary_complete } set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_stage = 5 } }
		add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_gui_dirty = 0.01 }
	}
}

# Called after completed work and daily; time alone can never unlock a chapter.
SRC_check_wartime_army_stage_progress = {
	SRC_wartime_army_ensure_chapters = yes
	SRC_wartime_army_recount_stage_work = yes
	SRC_wartime_army_recalculate = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			SRC_wartime_army_mechanic_available = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_stage_event_pending }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_work_busy }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_summary_complete }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { SRC_wartime_army_ready_for_stage_two = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_chapter_2_event_fired } }
			set_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_chapter_2_event_fired
			set_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_stage_event_pending
			country_event = { id = SRC_wartime_army.7 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SRC_wartime_army_ready_for_stage_three = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_chapter_3_event_fired } }
			set_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_chapter_3_event_fired
			set_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_stage_event_pending
			country_event = { id = SRC_wartime_army.2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SRC_wartime_army_ready_for_stage_four = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_chapter_4_event_fired } }
			set_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_chapter_4_event_fired
			set_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_stage_event_pending
			country_event = { id = SRC_wartime_army.3 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SRC_wartime_army_ready_for_stage_five = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_chapter_5_event_fired } }
			set_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_chapter_5_event_fired
			set_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_stage_event_pending
			country_event = { id = SRC_wartime_army.8 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SRC_wartime_army_ready_for_war_summary = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_chapter_summary_event_fired } }
			set_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_chapter_summary_event_fired
			set_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_summary_event_fired
			set_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_stage_event_pending
			SRC_wartime_army_recalculate_traditions = yes
			country_event = { id = SRC_wartime_army.5 }
		}
	}
}


#============================================================
### merged from SRC_wartime_army_v2.txt
#============================================================
# Hidden axes: foundation -100 mass tradition / +100 professional officers.
# Authority -100 soldier autonomy / +100 party leadership.
# Visible construction values are independent of both axes.
SRC_wartime_army_clamp = {
	clamp_variable = { var = SRC_wartime_army_command_stage min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = SRC_wartime_army_training_stage min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = SRC_wartime_army_political_stage min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = SRC_wartime_army_supply_stage min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = SRC_army_foundation min = -100 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = SRC_army_authority min = -100 max = 100 }
	add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_gui_dirty = 0.01 }
}

SRC_wartime_army_recalculate = {
	set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_average = SRC_wartime_army_command_stage }
	add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_average = SRC_wartime_army_training_stage }
	add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_average = SRC_wartime_army_political_stage }
	add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_average = SRC_wartime_army_supply_stage }
	divide_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_average = 4 }

	set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_values_at_60 = 0 }
	if = { limit = { OR = { check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_command_stage > 60 } check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_command_stage = 60 } } } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_values_at_60 = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { OR = { check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_training_stage > 60 } check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_training_stage = 60 } } } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_values_at_60 = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { OR = { check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_political_stage > 60 } check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_political_stage = 60 } } } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_values_at_60 = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { OR = { check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_supply_stage > 60 } check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_supply_stage = 60 } } } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_values_at_60 = 1 } }
}

SRC_wartime_army_remove_stage_spirits = {
	if = { limit = { has_idea = SRC_improvised_revolutionary_force } remove_ideas = SRC_improvised_revolutionary_force }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = SRC_initially_integrated_red_army } remove_ideas = SRC_initially_integrated_red_army }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = SRC_formed_northeast_red_army } remove_ideas = SRC_formed_northeast_red_army }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = SRC_northeast_red_army } remove_ideas = SRC_northeast_red_army }
}

SRC_wartime_army_remove_final_spirits = {
	if = { limit = { has_idea = SRC_revolutionary_regular_army } remove_ideas = SRC_revolutionary_regular_army }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = SRC_party_led_army } remove_ideas = SRC_party_led_army }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = SRC_armed_people } remove_ideas = SRC_armed_people }
}

SRC_wartime_army_refresh_spirit = {
	SRC_wartime_army_clamp = yes
	SRC_wartime_army_recalculate = yes
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { SRC_wartime_army_has_final_spirit = yes } }
		if = {
			limit = { SRC_wartime_army_ready_for_northeast_red_army = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_idea = SRC_northeast_red_army } }
				SRC_wartime_army_remove_stage_spirits = yes
				add_ideas = SRC_northeast_red_army
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SRC_wartime_army_ready_for_formed_army = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_idea = SRC_formed_northeast_red_army } }
				SRC_wartime_army_remove_stage_spirits = yes
				add_ideas = SRC_formed_northeast_red_army
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { SRC_wartime_army_ready_for_initial_integration = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_idea = SRC_initially_integrated_red_army } }
				SRC_wartime_army_remove_stage_spirits = yes
				add_ideas = SRC_initially_integrated_red_army
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_idea = SRC_improvised_revolutionary_force } }
				SRC_wartime_army_remove_stage_spirits = yes
				add_ideas = SRC_improvised_revolutionary_force
			}
		}
	}
	add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_gui_dirty = 0.01 }
}

SRC_wartime_army_gain_retain_officers = {
	add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_command_stage = 10 }
	add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_training_stage = 5 }
	add_to_variable = { SRC_army_foundation = 20 }
}

SRC_wartime_army_gain_field_staff_office = {

	add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_command_stage = 12 }
	add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_training_stage = 3 }
	add_to_variable = { SRC_army_foundation = 8 }
}

SRC_wartime_army_gain_veteran_training_units = {
	add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_training_stage = 10 }
	add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_command_stage = 3 }
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { check_variable = { SRC_army_foundation > 10 } check_variable = { SRC_army_foundation = 10 } } }
		add_to_variable = { SRC_army_foundation = 4 }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { SRC_army_foundation = -4 }
	}
}

}

SRC_wartime_army_clear_command_arrangement = {
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_arrangement_commanders_modifier } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_arrangement_commanders_modifier } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_arrangement_party_modifier } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_arrangement_party_modifier } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_arrangement_representatives_modifier } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_arrangement_representatives_modifier } }
	clr_country_flag = SRC_command_arrangement_commanders
	clr_country_flag = SRC_command_arrangement_party
	clr_country_flag = SRC_command_arrangement_representatives
}

SRC_wartime_army_begin_arrangement_change = {
	if = {
		limit = { SRC_wartime_army_has_command_arrangement = yes }
		set_country_flag = SRC_arrangement_switch_in_progress
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_arrangement_transition_modifier days = 30 }
		country_event = { id = SRC_wartime_army.6 days = 1 }
	}
}

SRC_wartime_army_adopt_commanders_arrangement = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = SRC_command_arrangement_representatives
				check_variable = { SRC_army_authority < -15 }
				check_variable = { SRC_army_authority = -15 }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = SRC_commanders_arrangement_unrest_pending
	}
	SRC_wartime_army_begin_arrangement_change = yes
	SRC_wartime_army_clear_command_arrangement = yes
	set_country_flag = SRC_command_arrangement_commanders
	set_country_flag = { flag = SRC_command_arrangement_cooldown days = 90 value = 1 }
	add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_arrangement_commanders_modifier }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = SRC_strengthened_military_commanders } }
		SRC_wartime_army_gain_strengthen_commanders = yes
		set_country_flag = SRC_strengthened_military_commanders
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_arrangement_switch_in_progress }
		subtract_from_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_political_stage = 3 }
		clr_country_flag = SRC_arrangement_switch_in_progress
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_commanders_arrangement_unrest_pending }
		clr_country_flag = SRC_commanders_arrangement_unrest_pending
		country_event = { id = SRC_wartime_army.4 days = 1 }
	}
	SRC_wartime_army_refresh_spirit = yes
}

SRC_wartime_army_adopt_party_arrangement = {
	SRC_wartime_army_begin_arrangement_change = yes
	SRC_wartime_army_clear_command_arrangement = yes
	set_country_flag = SRC_command_arrangement_party
	set_country_flag = { flag = SRC_command_arrangement_cooldown days = 90 value = 1 }
	add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_arrangement_party_modifier }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = SRC_expanded_party_network_in_units } }
		SRC_wartime_army_gain_expand_party_network = yes
		set_country_flag = SRC_expanded_party_network_in_units
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_arrangement_switch_in_progress }
		subtract_from_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_command_stage = 3 }
		clr_country_flag = SRC_arrangement_switch_in_progress
	}
	SRC_wartime_army_refresh_spirit = yes
}

SRC_wartime_army_adopt_representatives_arrangement = {
	SRC_wartime_army_begin_arrangement_change = yes
	SRC_wartime_army_clear_command_arrangement = yes
	set_country_flag = SRC_command_arrangement_representatives
	set_country_flag = { flag = SRC_command_arrangement_cooldown days = 90 value = 1 }
	add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_arrangement_representatives_modifier }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = SRC_expanded_soldier_representatives } }
		SRC_wartime_army_gain_expand_soldier_representatives = yes
		set_country_flag = SRC_expanded_soldier_representatives
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_arrangement_switch_in_progress }
		subtract_from_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_command_stage = 2 }
		subtract_from_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_training_stage = 2 }
		clr_country_flag = SRC_arrangement_switch_in_progress
	}
	SRC_wartime_army_refresh_spirit = yes
}

SRC_wartime_army_gain_general_headquarters = {
	add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_command_stage = 10 }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_command_stage < 55 } } set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_command_stage = 55 } }
	if = { limit = { OR = { check_variable = { SRC_army_foundation > 15 } check_variable = { SRC_army_foundation = 15 } } } army_experience = 10 }
	if = { limit = { OR = { check_variable = { SRC_army_authority > 15 } check_variable = { SRC_army_authority = 15 } } } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_political_stage = 3 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_general_headquarters_modifier } } } add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_general_headquarters_modifier } }
}

}

SRC_wartime_army_gain_unified_supply_department = {
	add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_supply_stage = 12 }
	if = { limit = { controls_state = 846 } add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_supply_stage = 2 } add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 300 } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_supply_stage < 55 } } set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_supply_stage = 55 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_unified_supply_modifier } } } add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_unified_supply_modifier } }
}

SRC_wartime_army_recalculate_traditions = {
	set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_professional_score = SRC_army_foundation }
	set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_party_score = SRC_army_authority }
	set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_people_score = 0 }
	subtract_from_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_people_score = SRC_army_foundation }
	subtract_from_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_people_score = SRC_army_authority }
}

SRC_wartime_army_remove_support_modifiers = {
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_mobilisation } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_mobilisation } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_temporary_discipline_modifier } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_temporary_discipline_modifier } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_armament_registry_modifier } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_armament_registry_modifier } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_field_command_modifier } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_field_command_modifier } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_field_repair_modifier } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_field_repair_modifier } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_railway_priority_modifier } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_railway_priority_modifier } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_grassroots_mobilisation } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_grassroots_mobilisation } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_general_headquarters_modifier } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_general_headquarters_modifier } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_permanent_training_modifier } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_permanent_training_modifier } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_political_reorganisation_modifier } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_political_reorganisation_modifier } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_unified_supply_modifier } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_unified_supply_modifier } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_arrangement_transition_modifier } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_wartime_army_arrangement_transition_modifier } }
	SRC_wartime_army_clear_command_arrangement = yes
	clr_country_flag = SRC_command_arrangement_cooldown
}

SRC_wartime_army_apply_regular_final = {
	SRC_wartime_army_remove_stage_spirits = yes
	SRC_wartime_army_remove_final_spirits = yes
	SRC_wartime_army_remove_support_modifiers = yes
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_hongjun } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_hongjun } }
	add_ideas = SRC_revolutionary_regular_army
	set_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_finalized
	add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_gui_dirty = 0.01 }
}

SRC_wartime_army_apply_party_final = {
	SRC_wartime_army_remove_stage_spirits = yes
	SRC_wartime_army_remove_final_spirits = yes
	SRC_wartime_army_remove_support_modifiers = yes
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_hongjun } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_hongjun } }
	add_ideas = SRC_party_led_army
	set_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_finalized
	add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_gui_dirty = 0.01 }
}

SRC_wartime_army_apply_people_final = {
	SRC_wartime_army_remove_stage_spirits = yes
	SRC_wartime_army_remove_final_spirits = yes
	SRC_wartime_army_remove_support_modifiers = yes
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_hongjun } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = SRC_hongjun } }
	add_ideas = SRC_armed_people
	set_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_finalized
	add_to_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_gui_dirty = 0.01 }
}

SRC_wartime_army_finalize_completed_route = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			SRC_wartime_army_ready_for_final_form = yes
			NOT = { SRC_wartime_army_has_final_spirit = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = SRC_wuzhuangliliangzhengguihua }
			SRC_wartime_army_apply_regular_final = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = SRC_gemingjunxinzuofeng
				has_completed_focus = SRC_quelidangweizhi
			}
			SRC_wartime_army_apply_party_final = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_completed_focus = SRC_gemingjunxinzuofeng
				has_completed_focus = SRC_fuquanshibingweiyuanhui
			}
			SRC_wartime_army_apply_people_final = yes
		}
	}
}

# One-time migration reconstructs progress from recorded choices, never grants rewards again.
SRC_wartime_army_ensure_v2 = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_v2_initialized } }
		set_variable = { SRC_army_foundation = 0 }
		set_variable = { SRC_army_authority = 0 }
		set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_command_stage = 25 }
		set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_training_stage = 25 }
		set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_political_stage = 20 }
		set_variable = { SRC_wartime_army_supply_stage = 20 }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_retain_officers_done } SRC_wartime_army_gain_retain_officers = yes }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_party_cells_done } SRC_wartime_army_gain_party_cells = yes }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_soldier_committee_done } SRC_wartime_army_gain_soldier_committee = yes }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_supply_network_done } SRC_wartime_army_gain_supply_network = yes }
		clear_variable = SRC_professional_officer_influence
		clear_variable = SRC_political_commissar_influence
		clear_variable = SRC_soldier_council_influence
		set_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_stages_initialized
		set_country_flag = SRC_wartime_army_v2_initialized
		SRC_wartime_army_clamp = yes
		SRC_wartime_army_recalculate = yes
	}
}

    }

        set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_influence_chen = SRC_xiyu_influence_chen }
        set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_support_ren = SRC_xiyu_support_ren }
        set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_support_li = SRC_xiyu_support_li }
        set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_support_chen = SRC_xiyu_support_chen }
        set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_cadre_term = SRC_xiyu_cadre_term }
    }
    else = {
        set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_influence_ren = SRC_huabei_influence_ren }
        set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_influence_li = SRC_huabei_influence_li }
        set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_influence_chen = SRC_huabei_influence_chen }
        set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_support_ren = SRC_huabei_support_ren }
        set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_support_li = SRC_huabei_support_li }
        set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_support_chen = SRC_huabei_support_chen }
        set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_cadre_term = SRC_huabei_cadre_term }
    }

    SRC_calc_current_bureau_influence_pie_progress = yes
    SRC_set_current_bureau_dominant_route = yes
    add_to_variable = { SRC_unified_gui_dirty = 0.01 }
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_zhongyangju_tab_dongbei } SRC_local_refresh_selected = yes }
}

SRC_calc_current_bureau_influence_pie_progress = {
    set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_pie_frame_ren = SRC_current_bureau_influence_ren }
    round_variable = SRC_current_bureau_pie_frame_ren

    set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_pie_start_li = SRC_current_bureau_influence_ren }
    round_variable = SRC_current_bureau_pie_start_li
    set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_pie_end_li = SRC_current_bureau_influence_ren }
    add_to_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_pie_end_li = SRC_current_bureau_influence_li }
    round_variable = SRC_current_bureau_pie_end_li
    set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_pie_frame_li = SRC_current_bureau_pie_start_li }
    multiply_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_pie_frame_li = 1000 }
    add_to_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_pie_frame_li = SRC_current_bureau_pie_end_li }

    set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_pie_start_chen = SRC_current_bureau_pie_end_li }
    set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_pie_end_chen = SRC_current_bureau_pie_end_li }
    add_to_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_pie_end_chen = SRC_current_bureau_influence_chen }
    round_variable = SRC_current_bureau_pie_start_chen
    round_variable = SRC_current_bureau_pie_end_chen
    set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_pie_frame_chen = SRC_current_bureau_pie_start_chen }
    multiply_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_pie_frame_chen = 1000 }
    add_to_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_pie_frame_chen = SRC_current_bureau_pie_end_chen }
}

SRC_set_current_bureau_dominant_route = {
    set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_dominant_route_id = 1 }
    set_temp_variable = { SRC_li_vs_ren = SRC_current_bureau_influence_li }
    subtract_from_temp_variable = { SRC_li_vs_ren = SRC_current_bureau_influence_ren }
    set_temp_variable = { SRC_li_vs_chen = SRC_current_bureau_influence_li }
    subtract_from_temp_variable = { SRC_li_vs_chen = SRC_current_bureau_influence_chen }
    set_temp_variable = { SRC_chen_vs_ren = SRC_current_bureau_influence_chen }
    subtract_from_temp_variable = { SRC_chen_vs_ren = SRC_current_bureau_influence_ren }
    set_temp_variable = { SRC_chen_vs_li = SRC_current_bureau_influence_chen }
    subtract_from_temp_variable = { SRC_chen_vs_li = SRC_current_bureau_influence_li }
    if = {
        limit = {
            check_variable = { SRC_li_vs_ren > 0 }
            NOT = { check_variable = { SRC_li_vs_chen < 0 } }
        }
        set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_dominant_route_id = 2 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            check_variable = { SRC_chen_vs_ren > 0 }
            check_variable = { SRC_chen_vs_li > 0 }
        }
        set_variable = { SRC_current_bureau_dominant_route_id = 3 }
    }
}

        clamp_variable = { var = SRC_dongnan_influence_chen min = 0 max = 100 }
        clamp_variable = { var = SRC_dongnan_support_ren min = 0 max = 100 }
        clamp_variable = { var = SRC_dongnan_support_li min = 0 max = 100 }
        clamp_variable = { var = SRC_dongnan_support_chen min = 0 max = 100 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = SRC_zhongyangju_tab_zhongnan }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_zhongnan_influence_ren = 2 }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_zhongnan_influence_li = 2 }
        add_to_variable = { SRC_zhongnan_influence_chen = 4 }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_zhongnan_support_ren = 4 }
        subtract_from_variable = { SRC_zhongnan_support_li = 4 }
        add_to_variable = { SRC_zhongnan_support_chen = 10 }
        clamp_variable = { var = SRC_zhongnan_influence_ren min = 0 max = 100 }
        clamp_variable = { var = SRC_zhongnan_influence_li min = 0 max = 100 }
        clamp_variable = { var = SRC_zhongnan_influence_chen min = 0 max = 100 }
        clamp_variable = { var = SRC_zhongnan_support_ren min = 0 max = 100 }
        clamp_variable = { var = SRC_zhongnan_support_li min = 0 max = 100 }
        clamp_variable = { var = SRC_zhongnan_support_chen min = 0 max = 100 }
    }

        clamp_variable = { var = SRC_xinan_influence_ren min = 0 max = 100 }
        clamp_variable = { var = SRC_xinan_influence_li min = 0 max = 100 }
        clamp_variable = { var = SRC_xinan_influence_chen min = 0 max = 100 }
        clamp_variable = { var = SRC_xinan_support_ren min = 0 max = 100 }

SRC_apply_current_route_to_selected_bureau = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_completed_focus = SRC_renfuchenzhuxi }
        SRC_shift_selected_bureau_toward_ren = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_completed_focus = SRC_lidazhaozhuxi }
        SRC_shift_selected_bureau_toward_li = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_completed_focus = SRC_chenduxiuzhuxi }
        SRC_shift_selected_bureau_toward_chen = yes
    }
}

SRC_register_huabei_influence_to_central = {
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = SRC_huabei_influence_registered } }
        set_country_flag = SRC_huabei_influence_registered
        set_temp_variable = { SRC_power_push = SRC_huabei_influence_ren }
        multiply_temp_variable = { SRC_power_push = 0.14 }
        add_to_variable = { SRC_xianfeng = SRC_power_push }
        set_temp_variable = { SRC_power_push = SRC_huabei_influence_li }
        multiply_temp_variable = { SRC_power_push = 0.14 }
        add_to_variable = { SRC_weiyuan = SRC_power_push }
        set_temp_variable = { SRC_power_push = SRC_huabei_influence_chen }
        multiply_temp_variable = { SRC_power_push = 0.14 }
        add_to_variable = { SRC_wenhe = SRC_power_push }
        SRC_power_struggle_normalize_balance = yes
    }
}

SRC_activity_enemy_worker_disruption_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_completed_focus = SRC_renfuchenzhuxi }
        every_enemy_country = {
            random_owned_controlled_state = {
                damage_building = {
                    type = infrastructure
                    damage = 2
                }
            }
            add_timed_idea = { idea = SRC_activity_enemy_disruption_strong days = 90 }
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_completed_focus = SRC_lidazhaozhuxi }
        every_enemy_country = {
            random_owned_controlled_state = {
                damage_building = {
                    type = infrastructure
                    damage = 1
                }
            }
            add_timed_idea = { idea = SRC_activity_enemy_disruption_medium days = 75 }
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_completed_focus = SRC_chenduxiuzhuxi }
        every_enemy_country = {
            random_owned_controlled_state = {
                damage_building = {
                    type = infrastructure
                    damage = 1
                }
            }
            add_timed_idea = { idea = SRC_activity_enemy_disruption_weak days = 60 }
        }
    }
}

SRC_activity_worker_movement_effect = {
    add_timed_idea = { idea = SRC_activity_worker_movement_buff days = 90 }
    SRC_activity_enemy_worker_disruption_effect = yes
}

SRC_activity_underground_network_effect = {
    add_manpower = 60000
    add_timed_idea = { idea = SRC_activity_underground_network_buff days = 120 }
    every_enemy_country = {
        add_timed_idea = { idea = SRC_activity_enemy_underground_disruption days = 75 }
    }
}

SRC_activity_revolutionary_government_effect = {
    add_timed_idea = { idea = SRC_activity_revolutionary_government_buff days = 120 }
    add_tech_bonus = {
        name = SRC_activity_revolutionary_government_nuclear_bonus
        bonus = 0.5
        uses = 1
        category = nuclear
    }
    if = {
        limit = { has_completed_focus = SRC_renfuchenzhuxi }
        add_popularity = { ideology = vanguard_socialism popularity = 0.03 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_completed_focus = SRC_lidazhaozhuxi }
        add_popularity = { ideology = council_socialism popularity = 0.03 }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { has_completed_focus = SRC_chenduxiuzhuxi }
        add_popularity = { ideology = moderate_socialism popularity = 0.03 }
    }
}

SRC_unlock_guerrilla_template_effect = {
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = SRC_guerrilla_template_unlocked } }
        set_country_flag = SRC_guerrilla_template_unlocked
        division_template = {
            name = "SRC_guerrilla_detachment"
            priority = 1
            division_names_group = MNG_INF_SHE
            regiments = {
                militia = { x = 0 y = 0 }
                militia = { x = 0 y = 1 }
                militia = { x = 0 y = 2 }
                militia = { x = 1 y = 0 }
                militia = { x = 1 y = 1 }
                militia = { x = 1 y = 2 }
            }
            support = {
                engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 }
            }
        }
    }
}

        event_target:SRC_local_action_state = { SRC_la_apply_worker_state = yes }
        SRC_local_refresh_selected = yes
    }

    }
    else = {
        SRC_power_struggle_calc_tangle_xy = yes
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            NOT = { has_country_flag = SRC_power_struggle_selected_ren }
            NOT = { has_country_flag = SRC_power_struggle_selected_li }
            NOT = { has_country_flag = SRC_power_struggle_selected_chen }

    set_variable = { SRC_unified_tab_frame_bureau = 1 }
    add_to_variable = { SRC_unified_gui_dirty = 0.01 }
}
